# Changelog — v0.x

Archived releases. The current major is in [the changelog](../changelog).

Releases before `v0.15.0` are not listed: their commits are no longer
reachable from `main` (the history was rewritten). They remain on the
[GitHub releases page](https://github.com/SocialGouv/iterion/releases).

## [0.51.0](https://github.com/SocialGouv/iterion/compare/v0.50.0...v0.51.0) (2026-07-19)

### Features

* **config-editor:** editable cadence + custom title + mint category guard ([03e19e3](https://github.com/SocialGouv/iterion/commit/03e19e3a1c330ea07b4a3a41a43ae40ac61e26bf))
